Helper functions in part A:


Careful: String.raw content can't include backticks; JSON.stringify with double quotes inside is fine (it's inside a String.raw template, but the double quotes are literal chars — fine).

Let me build an expression helper:
```js
function bodyText() { return cdp.evaluate("document.body.innerText"); }
function paneText(name) { return cdp.evaluate("(document.querySelector('[data-shot=\"" + name + "\"]')?.innerText ?? '')"); }
```
Hmm quoting inside String.raw: `'[data-shot=\"' ` — in String.raw, `\"` stays as backslash-quote in the output file! That's a problem: String.raw does not process escapes. So writing `\"` inside the raw template produces literal `\"` in the file. Inside a JS string literal in the generated file, `\"` is an escaped quote — valid JS! e.g. `"...[data-shot=\"intake\"]..."` in the file is fine JS. OK so `\"` is safe if placed inside a JS double-quoted string in the generated code.

But simpler: build selector expressions using single quotes and concatenation, avoiding escapes:
```js
const SEL = (name) => "document.querySelector('[data-shot=\"" + name + "\"]')";
```
still escapes. Alternative: use `document.querySelectorAll` and index? Simpler: use attribute selector without quotes: `[data-shot=intake]` is valid CSS when the value is a valid identifier (letters, digits, hyphens) — yes, `intake`, `run`, `use-demo`, `live-reasoning` are all valid identifiers. So write `'[data-shot=intake]'` — no escaping needed. 

For clicking buttons by text: 
```js
function clickByText(label) {
  return cdp.evaluate("(function(){var b=Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('button')).filter(function(x){return x.textContent.trim()==='" + label + "'})[0];if(!b)return false;b.click();return true;})()");
}
```
This embeds label in single quotes — labels contain no single quotes. Note '✓ 技术面试官' contains ✓ which is fine.

Setting React textarea value:
```js
function setTextarea(index, text) {
  return cdp.evaluate("(function(){var t=document.querySelectorAll('textarea')[" + index + "];var d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype,'value');d.set.call(t," + JSON.stringify(text) + ");t.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));return t.value.length;})()");
}
```
JSON.stringify produces a valid JS string literal with escapes — safe. But in String.raw generation, JSON.stringify is executed at runtime in the script, not at write time. Good.

For input:

```js
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { mkdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { ROOT, NL, sleep, log, startServer, startBrowser, waitForHttp, waitForDevtools, connect, makeChecker } from './vis-lib.mjs';

const PORT = 8821;
const CDP_PORT = 9345;
const OUT = join(ROOT, '.tmp', 'visible');
const DB = join(ROOT, '.tmp', 'visible', 'a.db');
const BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + String(PORT);

const { failed, check } = makeChecker();
let server = null; let browser = null; let cdp = null;

function clean() { ...kill... }

try {
  mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });
  for (const suffix of ['', '-wal', '-shm']) { try { rmSync(DB + suffix); } catch {} }
  server = startServer(PORT, DB, 'replay');
  await waitForHttp(BASE + '/api/health', 30000);
  browser = startBrowser(CDP_PORT, join(OUT, 'profile-a'));
  const wsUrl = await waitForDevtools(CDP_PORT, 30000);
  cdp = await connect(wsUrl);
  await cdp.send('Page.enable');
  await cdp.send('Runtime.enable');
  await cdp.send('Log.enable');
  await cdp.send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: 1600, height: 1000, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: false });
  await cdp.send('Page.navigate', { url: BASE });
  await cdp.waitFor("document.querySelector('[data-shot=intake]') !== null", '投料台出现', 20000);
  await sleep(600);
  cdp.events.length = 0;
  ...
```

Let me update vis-lib.mjs.Cdp.send with timeout:

```js
  send(method, params, timeoutMs) {
    const id = this.nextId;
    this.nextId += 1;
    const limit = timeoutMs === undefined ? 20000 : timeoutMs;
    return new Promise((resolve_, reject) => {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => { this.pending.delete(id); reject(new Error('调试调用超时：' + method)); }, limit);
      this.pending.set(id, { resolve: (v) => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve_(v); }, reject: (e) => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(e); } });
      this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params: params ?? {} }));
    });
  }
```
Note the close handler iterates pending values and calls slot.reject — still fine.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Key line 53: `if (/\s/.test(ch))` — in JavaScript, `\s` **does** match U+00A0 (NBSP is included in \s per ECMAScript: WhiteSpace includes <NBSP>). So NBSP is treated as whitespace and folded to a single space in normalization. So NBSP is handled by the fallback path (exact=false). Good — so NBSP is not the cause of 11/12 unsupported.

But note: the fallback maps NBSP runs to ' ' and the *quote* must also normalize the same way — consistent. So a quote with a normal space where source has NBSP would normalize both to ' ' and match. Fine. So anchoring is robust.

Then why 11 unsupported? Because with P1-1 changes, `enforceVerdict` downgrades solid/contradicts to unsupported when the model gives no resolvable evidenceQuotes. If the real model (reasoner) returned kind 'unsupported' directly, that's its own judgment. Or it returned solid but no evidence → downgrade.

I need to distinguish these cases: the verdicts event payload has `adjusted`/`adjustmentReason` fields. Let me query the DB aggregates: for each verdict, kind + whether adjustmentReason is non-empty + rationale length + evidence count. That's aggregate-ish, safe to print (adjustmentReason text may be generic like "模型判为「站得住」但拿不出可定位到简历原文的证据" — generic, safe).

But wait: the b.db was overwritten by the rerun; the latest rerun's DB should exist at .tmp/visible/b.db (script deletes it at start then the server creates). The rerun finished, server killed. The DB file remains. Consider the semantics from the product: 站得住 (solid) = 原文支持且经得起追问; 表述过度 (overstated) = 数字/范围夸大; 证据缺失 (unsupported) = 主张缺乏证据/口径; 与原文矛盾 (contradicts); 无法回答 (unanswerable) = 候选人答不上来.

For a real resume where the candidate (simulated from the resume only) can't provide details, "证据缺失" for most claims is arguably the honest judgement — the resume asserts something general and the candidate couldn't substantiate. <details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I understand. Note a bug in the prompt: there are two items numbered "4." (line 241 and 242). Minor cosmetic bug — the second "4." should be "5.". That's a real (if tiny) defect worth fixing.

Now, the口径: the prompt tells the model "拿不准时判 unsupported". So 11/12 unsupported on a real resume, where the candidate (simulated) says "简历里没写口径" — that's consistent with the definition. The tension I noted ("证据缺失" with evidence quotes) — actually evidenceQuotes are quotes that support the judgment; for unsupported, the evidence quote would be the vague résumé line which justifies "只有结论没有口径". So it's actually coherent: evidence quote = the sentence that is itself unsupported. Hmm, the prompt says "evidenceQuotes 必须是简历原文或候选人回答里的逐字片段，用来支撑你的判定" — so citing the vague line supports "unsupported". OK, so not a defect per se. But the report UI says "没有可援引的简历原文——这正是它被判为「…」的原因" when evidence is empty for that kind; with evidence present, it lists quotes. Fine.

However, the real issue for a resume tool: if nearly everything is judged 证据缺失, the demo is less informative. But honesty-wise it's defensible: a real resume has no metric details. The product's value is exactly to surface that.

But wait — 2 solid in run 1 and 0 in run 2, all others unsupported, ~0 unanswerable. Since the simulated candidate answers from the resume only, "候选人答不上来" cases are rare — the simulation almost always says "简历里没写" → unsupported by the prompt rule. That's consistent.

One thing to consider: the P1-1 change made "答不上来" less likely to become unanswerable. Actually the code change was: 只把整场未答的 solid 降级 unsupported. Fine.

So conclusion for P1-1: no new code change strictly required. But there IS a genuine improvement: the duplicated numbering "4." in VERDICT_SYSTEM (lines 241/242) — fix to 5. Also the definition for `unsupported` mentions 责任在简历写法, good.

```text
495| 2. `deepseek-reasoner` 支持 `response_format: {type:'json_object'}`，因此它可以替换任何一个
496|    本来就要求 JSON 输出的阶段，不需要为它单独设计提示词。
497| 3. 流式下思考在 `delta.reasoning_content`、正文在 `delta.content`；一次实测 491 字符思考 /
498|    64 字符正文 / 2389 ms，usage 里有 `completion_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens` 与 `prompt_cache_hit_tokens`。
499| 4. `deepseek-chat` 的 reasoning 恒为 0 字符——不接思考模型就真的没有思考，界面那行斜体不会凭空出现。
500| 
501| 一条必须如实写进交付物的代价：**思考文本通常是英文**（例如 The user asks: one sentence explaining…）。
502| 这是模型侧行为，界面上会出现中英混排。我们选择不翻译：翻译会把「模型的思考」变成「我们的复述」，
503| 而第 2 章刚论证过「不编造」正是这个产品的价值。
504| 
505| **教训**：接第三方模型前，先把「它的名字从哪来、能力边界在哪、输出长什么样」实测一遍。
506| 模型清单、官方文档、别人的博客都不是证据。
507| 
508| ## F34. 验收脚本的三种假信号：恒真条件、过早成立的条件、从别的测试抄来的期望值（2026-10-26）
509| 
510| 报告导出的真机验收脚本（12 项 PASS/FAIL）第一轮报了一项失败，查下去发现**产品没问题，是脚本在骗人**。三类假信号一次凑齐：
511|
```

Session 17 · 2026-10-26 · 可见浏览器逐功能点测（真机）

**目标**：把「每个功能都点一遍」从承诺变成一份可复核的记录：打开真实（可见）浏览器，用用户 2024 年的真实简历当样例，回放与联网各跑一遍。

### 做了什么
- 三个一次性脚本（.tmp，不入库）：vis-lib.mjs（找密钥 / 起服务 / 起可见 Edge / CDP 客户端 / 打点器）、vis-part-a.mjs（回放模式逐项）、vis-part-b.mjs（联网 + 真实简历）。
- 回放模式 46 项断言全过：空状态四条机制、简历太短的中文报错（界面 + 接口 400）、一键示例、五个面试官角色与人设、增删面试官时契约随之变化、慢放高亮、阶段时间线、实时输出逐字、思考行、五张主张卡片、判定条数与服务端一致、中文判定标签、代码收敛标注、改写建议、被锚定的主张被拦下、追问链与轮次、契约六行 + System Prompt + 「与运行时一致」、点引文右栏「聚焦 第 81–108 字」、遥测与「追问 10」、三种报告各真取一遍、重跑 409、未知接口 404、载回历史、删除、清空，控制台 0 报错。
- 联网 + 真实简历 18 项全过：真实简历 1007 字（含 11 个 U+00A0）贴进界面即可跑；跑完 29–31 s；12 条主张全部锚定成功（11 逐字 + 1 归一化）；12 条判定；17–18 次追问 / 3 轮；12 条建议；思考行最长 221 字；报告 Markdown 16.4–17.7 K 字符；控制台 0 报错。

### 抓到的两件事
- **工具缺陷（产品侧）**：`Page.captureScreenshot` 在可见浏览器下可以**永不返回**，连接与进程都还在，脚本永久卡住（跑第一遍 part A 时真的挂了 11 分钟，只能按端口杀进程）。处置：给截图器与界面点测两个工具的每条 CDP 命令加 20 秒超时 —— 最坏情况是少一张图，而不是整轮没有结论。
- **提示词缺陷（产品侧）**：VERDICT_SYSTEM 的纪律清单里有两个「4.」（编号重复），而这段提示词正是界面上「System Prompt」按钮展示的内容。改成 4/5。附带后果：提示词变一个字，5 盘 verdict 卡带指纹全变，必须重跑 `npm run seed:demo`（清理旧卡带 5 条、签名仍是 428b82589a88）——漂移门禁按设计生效，这条价值与代价一起写进 F36。

### 验证
- `npm run verify` exit 0（33 文件 / 329 断言 + 冒烟 9 项 + build）。
- `npm run e2e:ui` **33/33** 仍全过（改超时之后）。
- 两轮点测：回放 46/46、联网 18/18；控制台 0 报错。

### 下一步
方案 A 录演示视频（含 ≥2 分钟 AI 协同过程复现）→ 补 LICENSE 与发布清单 → 公开仓库地址。

F36: 提示词也在界面上，改一个字符就是一次全局漂移

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Interesting: commit subjects contain literal "\n" — because git commit -m with JSON.stringify... wait, in bash, JSON.stringify(msg) produces a double-quoted string with \n escapes; bash's `-m "line1\nline2"` passes a literal backslash-n, not a newline. So the commit message body is one line with literal \n. That's ugly and it pollutes the git log — and these messages are part of the submission evidence (evaluators read commit log!). I must fix the commit messages.

Wait — but earlier commits (from previous sessions) appear fine? Let me check an earlier commit, e.g. be0c7b3 shows `docs: 界面点测的断言数更新到 33 项` — single-line subject, fine. Earlier multi-line messages (e.g. 34da13e) — in the compressed summaries they were one-line subjects. Hmm, my earlier commits in this session... Let me check whether previous session commits had literal \n too. Looking at the log output above: top 4 commits have literal \n appended to subject. Older ones (be0c7b3, 5369305) show clean single-line subjects.

So I've been passing -m with escaped \n which becomes literal. That means the "subject" of my four commits is a long line containing "\n\n..." — very bad for a competition that judges commit quality.

Fix: amend the 4 commits with proper multi-line messages using a file (-F) or $'...' ANSI-C quoting in bash. Safest: write message to a temp file and use `git commit --amend -F file` in order (oldest first requires rebase; but amending the tip only changes the tip). Since all 4 are the last 4 commits, I can use `git rebase` — complex. Alternative: `git commit --amend` sequentially won't work for older ones.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Good. Tailwind v4 @theme with CSS variables. For a light theme, since colors are referenced via var(--color-ink-*), I can define a light override under `html.light` / `[data-theme="light"]` and re-map the ink scale + background. But careful: ink-950..100 are used both for backgrounds and text. In light mode, we want backgrounds light and text dark — meaning the scale must invert (ink-950 → light background, ink-100 → dark text). That works if semantics are "ink-950 = deepest background, ink-100 = primary text". Indeed: pane bg is ink-900, body bg ink-950, text ink-100, borders ink-800/700, muted text ink-300.

So for light theme: ink-950 = #f7f8fa (page bg), ink-900 = #ffffff (pane), ink-850 = #f1f3f7, ink-800 = #e3e7ee (border), ink-700 = #cfd6e2, ink-600 = #aab4c4, ink-500 = #7c8798, ink-400 = #5b6674, ink-300 = #4a5462 (secondary text), ink-200 = #2f3743, ink-100 = #161b22 (primary text).

Also ember/mint/amber/rose need darker variants for light bg contrast. And the badge backgrounds use rgba with alpha — those are fine but the text colors need darkening.

Also `.badge-primary` btn-primary text #1a0f04 on orange gradient — fine in both.

Also `mark.anchor-hit` bullet uses ink-100 color — in light mode that becomes dark text, good.

Implementation: in index.css add:
```css
@theme { ...existing... }

/* 浅色主题：同一套色阶语义取反（950 是底、100 是字） */
:root[data-theme='light'] {
  --color-ink-950: ...
  ...
}
```
Wait — Tailwind v4 `@theme` generates CSS custom properties on `:root`. Overriding them on `:root[data-theme='light']` works for anything using `var(--color-ink-*)` (utilities like `bg-ink-900` compile to `background-color: var(--color-ink-900)`, so overriding the variable changes them at runtime). Yes! In Tailwind v4, utilities reference the theme variables. Let me verify: v4 generates `.bg-ink-900 { background-color: var(--color-ink-900); }`. Yes, v4 uses var() references for theme colors. Also opacity modifiers like `bg-ink-900/70` compile to `color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-ink-900) 70%, transparent)` — works with overridden vars.

One caveat: `@theme` variables are emitted in `:root` — specificity of `:root[data-theme='light']` (0,1,1) beats `:root` (0,1,0). Good.

So theme switching = set `document.documentElement.dataset.theme = 'light' | 'dark'` and persist in localStorage. Plus body background-image gradients need a light variant (override in the light block).

Let me now check the badge rgba values: e.g. `.badge-solid { color: var(--color-mint-400); background: rgba(53,211,159,0.12) }`. In light mode, mint-400 must be dark enough: overridden to #0b7a5a. Fine.

`.quote` uses ember-300 for text → light: #9a4a12, background rgba(255,138,61,0.07) ok, border-left ember-500 → light: #d97316.

Also `.btn-primary { color: #1a0f04 }` fine.

Also scrollbar colors use ink-600/ink-700 → light values are light grays, ok.

```ts
export type Theme = 'dark' | 'light';
export const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'crossexam.theme';
export const THEME_LABEL: Record<Theme, string> = { dark: '深色', light: '浅色' };
export const DEFAULT_THEME: Theme = 'dark';

export function parseTheme(raw: string | null | undefined): Theme {
  return raw === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark';
}
export function nextTheme(current: Theme): Theme {
  return current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
}
export function readStoredTheme(): Theme {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return DEFAULT_THEME;
  try { return parseTheme(localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)); } catch { return DEFAULT_THEME; }
}
export function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
  document.documentElement.dataset['theme'] = theme;  // hmm: dataset index signature
}
export function saveTheme(theme: Theme): void { try { localStorage.setItem(...) } catch {} }
```

Let me write theme.ts content:

```ts
/**
 * 配色主题：深色是默认（发布出来的样子），浅色给白天、投影仪、打印稿看。
 *
 * 这里刻意只做一件事：把 data-theme 写到 <html> 上。
 * 颜色本身全在 index.css 的 :root[data-theme='light'] 里覆盖了同一套 --color-ink-* 变量，
 * 界面代码里一个「深色分支」都不需要写——写分支迟早会漏掉某个组件。
 */
export type Theme = 'dark' | 'light';

export const DEFAULT_THEME: Theme = 'dark';
export const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'crossexam.theme';
export const THEME_LABEL: Record<Theme, string> = { dark: '深色', light: '浅色' };

/** 认不出来的值一律当默认，不抛错：localStorage 里可能是上一个版本写的 */
export function parseTheme(raw: string | null | undefined): Theme {
  return raw === 'light' ? 'light' : DEFAULT_THEME;
}

export function nextTheme(current: Theme): Theme {
  return current === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light';
}

export function readStoredTheme(): Theme {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return DEFAULT_THEME;
  try {
    return parseTheme(localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY));
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_THEME;
  }
}

export function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
  document.documentElement.dataset['theme'] = theme;
}

export function saveTheme(theme: Theme): void {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme);
  } catch {
    // 隐私模式下写不进去也不该影响用界面
  }
}
```

- nextTheme both ways
- THEME_LABEL both
- readStoredTheme with no localStorage → default (in node env, unless Node 24 has it — risky). Repo convention forbids `as any` and `@ts-ignore`; `as unknown as X` is not forbidden (probably). Let me check if there are existing `as unknown as` usages... can't recall. ```ts

```ts
}
```
and pass a plain object `{ getItem: (key: string) => (key === THEME_STORAGE_KEY ? 'light' : null), setItem: (key: string, value: string) => { written.push(key + '=' + value); } }`. Since defineProperty takes unknown, no cast needed. 
- applyTheme with fake document: installStorage not needed; defineProperty 'document' with `{ documentElement: { dataset: {} } }`... but dataset typed as any? Since we're using Object.defineProperty(globalThis,'document',{value: fake}) with unknown value, no TS error. Then after applyTheme('light'), read (fake.documentElement.dataset as Record<string,string>)['theme'] — hmm, casting again. Instead keep a typed local object: `const dataset: Record<string, string> = {}; const fakeDoc = { documentElement: { dataset } };` → then read `dataset['theme']`. No cast needed. 

Also test that `document` undefined → applyTheme doesn't throw: defineProperty value undefined then call.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { applyTheme, DEFAULT_THEME, nextTheme, parseTheme, readStoredTheme, saveTheme, THEME_LABEL, THEME_STORAGE_KEY } from '../src/theme.ts';

function install(key: string, value: unknown): void {
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, key, { value, configurable: true, writable: true });
}

describe('配色主题', () => {
  it('只认 light，其余一律回默认', ...);
  it('两档来回切', ...);
  it('标签是中文', ...);
  it('没有 localStorage 时用默认值，不抛错', () => { install('localStorage', undefined); expect(readStoredTheme()).toBe('dark'); });
  it('读得出存下来的浅色', () => { const store = { getItem: (k) => (k === THEME_STORAGE_KEY ? 'light' : null) }; install('localStorage', store); expect(readStoredTheme()).toBe('light'); });
  it('localStorage 抛异常时回默认', () => { install('localStorage', { getItem: () => { throw new Error('关掉了'); } }); expect(readStoredTheme()).toBe('dark'); });
  it('applyTheme 只改 <html data-theme>', () => { const dataset: Record<string,string> = {}; install('document', { documentElement: { dataset } }); applyTheme('light'); expect(dataset['theme']).toBe('light'); applyTheme('dark'); expect(dataset['theme']).toBe('dark'); });
  it('没有 document 也不抛错', () => { install('document', undefined); expect(() => applyTheme('light')).not.toThrow(); });
  it('saveTheme 写进存储；写不进去也不抛错', () => { const written: string[] = []; install('localStorage', { setItem: (k,v) => written.push(k+'='+v) }); saveTheme('light'); expect(written).toEqual([THEME_STORAGE_KEY+'=light']); install('localStorage', { setItem: () => { throw new Error('满了'); } }); expect(() => saveTheme('light')).not.toThrow(); });
});
```

Careful: the write of the test file must not contain backticks (String.raw constraint). Use single quotes. Avoid `${`. OK.

Also cleanup: after tests, globals remain set (localStorage stub, document undefined). Since each test file runs in its own worker/module registry, and other web tests don't use localStorage/document, it should be fine. But `applyTheme('light')` with fake document is fine.

help.test.ts:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { FAQ, HELP_SEEN_KEY, HELP_STEPS, markHelpSeen, MODE_ROWS, PRIVACY_NOTE, shouldAutoOpenHelp } from '../src/help.ts';

function install(name: string, value: unknown): void { Object.defineProperty(globalThis, name, { value, configurable: true, writable: true }); }

describe('新手教程', () => {
  it('四步上手，每步都有标题和正文', ...);
  it('三种模式都列了，且只有回放不需要 Key', () => { expect(MODE_ROWS.map(r=>r.mode)).toEqual(['replay','record','live']); for (const row of MODE_ROWS) { expect(row.what.length).toBeGreaterThan(8); expect(row.when.length).toBeGreaterThan(6); expect(row.needsKey).toBe(row.mode === 'replay' ? '不需要' : '需要'); } });
  it('隐私口径同时说清「本地」和「联网会发给服务商」', () => { expect(PRIVACY_NOTE).toContain('SQLite'); expect(PRIVACY_NOTE).toContain('模型服务商'); expect(PRIVACY_NOTE).toContain('不联网'); });
  it('常见问题不少于四条且都有答案', ...);
  it('第一次打开自动弹教程，看过一次就不再弹', () => {
    install('localStorage', undefined);
    expect(shouldAutoOpenHelp()).toBe(false);
    const map = new Map<string,string>();
    install('localStorage', { getItem: (k:string) => map.get(k) ?? null, setItem: (k:string,v:string) => { map.set(k,v); } });
    expect(shouldAutoOpenHelp()).toBe(true);
    markHelpSeen();
    expect(map.get(HELP_SEEN_KEY)).toBe('1');
    expect(shouldAutoOpenHelp()).toBe(false);
  });
  it('存储读的时候抛异常就不弹，不把界面带崩', () => { install('localStorage', { getItem: () => { throw new Error('关掉了'); }, setItem: () => { throw new Error('关掉了'); } }); expect(shouldAutoOpenHelp()).toBe(false); expect(() => markHelpSeen()).not.toThrow(); });
});
```

Then run tsc + full vitest.
